<vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + element state\n            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];\n            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\",     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0}); n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// One thread per element. Connectivity is stored transposed (connection-major,\n// conn_idx[j * n + i]) so that consecutive threads read consecutive memory.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,       // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,         // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,    // per-element material coeff\n    const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,     // per-element material flow\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux_out) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_in[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow, then add flux from all connected elements in\n    // the same order as the sequential reference to preserve rounding.\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const uint32_t neighbor = conn_idx[j * n_elems + i];\n        const val_t neighbor_energy = energy_in[neighbor];\n        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_energy) * coeff *\n                      conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    energy_out[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    tflux_out[i] = tflux_in[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU. i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// One thread per element. Connectivity is stored transposed (connection-major,\n// conn_idx[j * n + i]) so that consecutive threads read consecutive memory.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,       // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,         // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,    // per-element material coeff\n    const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,     // per-element material flow\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux_out) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_in[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow, then add flux from all connected elements in\n    // the same order as the sequential reference to preserve rounding.\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const uint32_t neighbor = conn_idx[j * n_elems + i];\n        const val_t neighbor_energy = energy_in[neighbor];\n        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_energy) * coeff *\n                      conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    energy_out[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    tflux_out[i] = tflux_in[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU. The mesh is uploaded\n// once, all iterations run device-side with double buffering, and the final\n// state is copied back into world.elements_dynamic.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Only stage/upload connectivity rows that are actually populated.\n    uint32_t max_conn = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        max_conn = std::max(max_conn,\n                            static_cast<uint32_t>(world.elements_static[i].num_connections));\n    }\n\n    // Flatten static data into transposed (connection-major) SoA staging\n    // buffers and resolve each element's material to per-element\n    // coefficients. Pinned host memory keeps the H2D/D2H copies on the fast\n    // DMA path.\n    uint32_t* h_num_conn = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* h_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* h_conn_flux = nullptr;\n    val_t* h_coeff = nullptr;\n    val_t* h_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* h_energy = nullptr;\n    val_t* h_tflux = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_conn_idx, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_conn_flux, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_coeff, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_energy, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_tflux, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& es = world.elements_static[i];\n        const Material& mat = world.materials[es.material_idx];\n        h_num_conn[i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.num_connections);\n        for (idx_t j = 0; j < es.num_connections; ++j) {\n            h_conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.connected_idx[j]);\n            h_conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = es.connected_flux[j];\n        }\n        h_coeff[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n        h_extflow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n        h_energy[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n        h_tflux[i] = world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n    }\n\n    uint32_t* d_num_conn = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* d_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_conn_flux = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_coeff = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    val_t* d_tflux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_coeff, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_tflux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_num_conn, h_num_conn,\n                               n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_conn_idx, h_conn_idx,\n                               n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(uint32_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_conn_flux, h_conn_flux,\n                               n_elems * max_conn * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_coeff, h_coeff,\n                               n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_extflow, h_extflow,\n                               n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_energy[0], h_energy,\n                               n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_tflux[0], h_tflux,\n                               n_elems * sizeof(val_t), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const int block_size = 256;\n    const int grid_size = static_cast<int>((n_elems + block_size - 1) / block_size);\n\n    int cur = 0;\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n\n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// One thread per element. Connectivity is stored transposed (connection-major,\n// conn_idx[j * n + i]) so that consecutive threads read consecutive memory.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,       // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,         // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,    // per-element material coeff\n    const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,     // per-element material flow\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux_out) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_in[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow, then add flux from all connected elements in\n    // the same order as the sequential reference to preserve rounding.\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const uint32_t neighbor = conn_idx[j * n_elems + i];\n        const val_t neighbor_energy = energy_in[neighbor];\n        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_energy) * coeff *\n                      conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    energy_out[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    tflux_out[i] = tflux_in[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU. The mesh is uploaded\n// once, all iterations run device-side with double buffering, and the final\n// state is copied back into world.elements_dynamic.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Only stage/upload connectivity rows that are actually populated.\n    uint32_t max_conn = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        max_conn = std::max(max_conn,\n                            static_cast<uint32_t>(world.elements_static[i].num_connections));\n    }\n\n    // Flatten static data into transposed (connection-major) SoA staging\n    // buffers and resolve each element's material to per-element\n    // coefficients. sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_tflux, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        const ElementStatic& es = world.elements_static[i];\n world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n The mesh is uploaded\n// once, all iterations run device-side with double buffering, and the final\n// state is copied back into world.elements_dynamic.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n\n    // Only stage/upload connectivity rows that are actually populated.\n    uint32_t max_conn = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        max_conn = std::max(max_conn,\n                            static_cast<uint32_t>(world.elements_static[i].num_connections));\n    }\n\n    // Flatten static data into transposed (connection-major) SoA staging\n    // buffers and resolve each element's material to per-element\n    // coefficients. Pinned host memory keeps the H2D/D2H copies on the fast\n    // DMA path.\n    uint32_t* h_num_conn = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* h_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* h_conn_flux = nullptr;\n    val_t* <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements\nusing idx_t = uint64_t;\nusing val_t = double;\n\n// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)\nconstexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                      \\\n        cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                            \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);            \\\n            exit(1);                                                          \\\n        }                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Material properties for energy transfer\nstruct Material {\n    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Unpack the AoS mesh description into transposed (connection-major) SoA\n// arrays and resolve each element's material to per-element coefficients.\n// Runs once on the device so the host never has to restructure the mesh.\n__global__ void unpackMeshKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elements_static,\n    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,             // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,               // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,\n    val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& es = elements_static[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.num_connections);\n    num_connections[i] = n_conn;\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.connected_idx[j]);\n        conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = es.connected_flux[j];\n    }\n    const Material mat = materials[es.material_idx];\n    transfer_coeff[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n    external_flow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n    energy[i] = elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n    tflux[i] = elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n}\n\n// Pack the final SoA state back into the AoS layout expected by the host.\n__global__ void packResultKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux,\n    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n    elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = energy[i];\n    elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = tflux[i];\n}\n\n// One thread per element. Connectivity is stored transposed (connection-major,\n// conn_idx[j * n + i]) so that consecutive threads read consecutive memory.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,       // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,         // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,    // per-element material coeff\n    const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,     // per-element material flow\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux_out) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_in[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow, then add flux from all connected elements in\n    // the same order as the sequential reference to preserve rounding.\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const uint32_t neighbor = conn_idx[j * n_elems + i];\n        const val_t neighbor_energy = energy_in[neighbor];\n        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_energy) * coeff *\n                      conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    energy_out[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    tflux_out[i] = tflux_in[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU. The mesh is uploaded\n// once, all iterations run device-side with double buffering, and the final\n// state is copied back into world.elements_dynamic.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const size_t n_materials = world.materials.size();\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dynamic = 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Unpack the AoS mesh description into transposed (connection-major) SoA\n// arrays and resolve each element's material to per-element coefficients.\n// Runs once on the device so the host never has to restructure the mesh.\n__global__ void unpackMeshKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elements_static,\n    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,             // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,               // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,\n    val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& es = elements_static[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.num_connections);\n    num_connections[i] = n_conn;\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.connected_idx[j]);\n        conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = es.connected_flux[j];\n    }\n    const Material mat = materials[es.material_idx];\n    transfer_coeff[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n    external_flow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n    energy[i] = elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n    tflux[i] = elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n}\n\n// Pack the final SoA state back into the AoS layout expected by the host.\n__global__ void packResultKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux,\n    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n    elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = energy[i];\n    elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = tflux[i];\n}\n\n// One thread per element. Connectivity is stored transposed (connection-major,\n// conn_idx[j * n + i]) so that consecutive threads read consecutive memory.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,       // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,         // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,    // per-element material coeff\n    const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,     // per-element material flow\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux_out) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_in[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow, then add flux from all connected elements in\n    // the same order as the sequential reference to preserve rounding.\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const uint32_t neighbor = conn_idx[j * n_elems + i];\n        const val_t neighbor_energy = energy_in[neighbor];\n        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_energy) * coeff *\n                      conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    energy_out[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    tflux_out[i] = tflux_in[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU. The mesh is uploaded\n// once, all iterations run device-side with double buffering, and the final\n// state is copied back into world.elements_dynamic.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const size_t n_materials = world.materials.size();\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dynamic = nullptr;\n    Material* d_materials = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* d_num_conn = nullptr;\n    uint32_t* d_conn_idx = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_conn_flux = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_coeff = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    val_t* d_tflux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_static, n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dynamic, n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_materials, n_materials * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n_elems * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_coeff, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_tflux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    // Ship the mesh in its native AoS layout and let the GPU restructure it:\n    // the device-side transpose is far faster than flattening on the host.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_static, world.elements_static.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic.data(),\n                          n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_materials, world.materials.data(),\n                          n_materials * sizeof(Material), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    const int block_size = 256;\n    const int grid_size = static_cast<int>((n_elems + block_size - 1) / block_size);\n\n    unpackMeshKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(\n        n_elems, d_static, d_dynamic, d_materials, d_num_conn, d_conn_idx,\n        d_conn_flux, d_coeff, d_extflow, d_energy[0], d_tflux[0]);\n\n    int cur = 0;\n    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n        simulationStepKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(\n            n_elems, d_num_conn, d_conn_idx, d_conn_flux, d_coeff, d_extflow,\n            d_energy[cur], d_tflux[cur], d_energy[1 - cur], d_tflux[1 - cur]);\n        cur = 1 - cur;\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    packResultKernel<<<grid_size, block_size>>>(\n        n_elems, d_energy[cur], d_tflux[cur], d_dynamic);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), d_dynamic,\n                          n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_static));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_dynamic));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_materials));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_num_conn));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_idx));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_coeff));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_extflow));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_energy[b]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_tflux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n\n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements)   world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;"," }"," ","+// Copy between pageable host memory and the device through a small pinned","+// double buffer. This keeps transfers on the fast DMA path without paying the","+// cost of pinning the full-size host allocations.","+class ChunkedCopier {","+public:","+    static constexpr size_t CHUNK_BYTES = 16 << 20;","+","+    ChunkedCopier() {","+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&pinned_, 2 * CHUNK_BYTES));","+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams_[0]));","+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams_[1]));","+    }","+    ~ChunkedCopier() {","+        cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[0]);","+        cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[1]);","+        cudaFreeHost(pinned_);","+    }","+    ChunkedCopier(const ChunkedCopier&) = delete;","+    ChunkedCopier& operator=(const ChunkedCopier&) = delete;","+","+    void hostToDevice(void* dst, const void* src, size_t bytes) {","+        int b = 0;","+        for (size_t off = 0; off < bytes; off += CHUNK_BYTES, b ^= 1) {","+            const size_t c = std::min(bytes - off, CHUNK_BYTES);","+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b]));","+            memcpy(pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES, static_cast<const char*>(src) + off, c);","+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(static_cast<char*>(dst) + off,","+                                       pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES, c,","+                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams_[b]));","+        }","+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[0]));","+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[1]));","+    }","+","+    void deviceToHost(void* dst, const void* src, size_t bytes) {","+        // Pipeline: issue async D2H into one pinned buffer while draining the","+        // other into the pageable destination.","+        size_t pending_off = 0, pending_size = 0;","+        int b = 0;","+        for (size_t off = 0; off < bytes; off += CHUNK_BYTES, b ^= 1) {","+            const size_t c = std::min(bytes - off, CHUNK_BYTES);","+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES,","+                                       static_cast<const char*>(src) + off, c,","+                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams_[b]));","+            if (pending_size > 0) {","+                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b ^ 1]));","+                memcpy(static_cast<char*>(dst) + pending_off,","+                       pinned_ + (b ^ 1) * CHUNK_BYTES, pending_size);","+            }","+            pending_off = off;","+            pending_size = c;","+        }","+        if (pending_size > 0) {","+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b ^ 1]));","+            memcpy(static_cast<char*>(dst) + pending_off,","+                   pinned_ + (b ^ 1) * CHUNK_BYTES, pending_size);","+        }","+    }","+","+private:","+    char* pinned_ = nullptr;","+    cudaStream_t streams_[2];","+};","+"," // Unpack the AoS mesh description into transposed (connection-major) SoA"," // arrays and resolve each element's material to per-element coefficients."," // Runs once on the device so the host never has to restructure the mesh."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Copy between pageable host memory and the device through a small pinned\n// double buffer. This keeps transfers on the fast DMA path without paying the\n// cost of pinning the full-size host allocations.\nclass ChunkedCopier {\npublic:\n    static constexpr size_t CHUNK_BYTES = 16 << 20;\n\n    ChunkedCopier() {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&pinned_, 2 * CHUNK_BYTES));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams_[0]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams_[1]));\n    }\n    ~ChunkedCopier() {\n        cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[0]);\n        cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[1]);\n        cudaFreeHost(pinned_);\n    }\n    ChunkedCopier(const ChunkedCopier&) = delete;\n    ChunkedCopier& operator=(const ChunkedCopier&) = delete;\n\n    void hostToDevice(void* dst, const void* src, size_t bytes) {\n        int b = 0;\n        for (size_t off = 0; off < bytes; off += CHUNK_BYTES, b ^= 1) {\n            const size_t c = std::min(bytes - off, CHUNK_BYTES);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b]));\n            memcpy(pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES, static_cast<const char*>(src) + off, c);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(static_cast<char*>(dst) + off,\n                                       pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES, c,\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams_[b]));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[0]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[1]));\n    }\n\n    void deviceToHost(void* dst, const void* src, size_t bytes) {\n        // Pipeline: issue async D2H into one pinned buffer while draining the\n        // other into the pageable destination.\n        size_t pending_off = 0, pending_size = 0;\n        int b = 0;\n        for (size_t off = 0; off < bytes; off += CHUNK_BYTES, b ^= 1) {\n            const size_t c = std::min(bytes - off, CHUNK_BYTES);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES,\n                                       static_cast<const char*>(src) + off, c,\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams_[b]));\n            if (pending_size > 0) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b ^ 1]));\n                memcpy(static_cast<char*>(dst) + pending_off,\n                       pinned_ + (b ^ 1) * CHUNK_BYTES, pending_size);\n            }\n            pending_off = off;\n            pending_size = c;\n        }\n        if (pending_size > 0) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b ^ 1]));\n            memcpy(static_cast<char*>(dst) + pending_off,\n                   pinned_ + (b ^ 1) * CHUNK_BYTES, pending_size);\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    char* pinned_ = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t streams_[2];\n};\n\n// Unpack the AoS mesh description into transposed (connection-major) SoA\n// arrays and resolve each element's material to per-element coefficients.\n// Runs once on the device so the host never has to restructure the mesh.\n__global__ void unpackMeshKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elements_static,\n    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,             // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,               // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,\n    val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& es = elements_static[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.num_connections);\n    num_connections[i] = n_conn;\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.connected_idx[j]);\n        conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = es.connected_flux[j];\n    }\n    const Material mat = materials[es.material_idx];\n    transfer_coeff[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n    external_flow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n    energy[i] = elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n    tflux[i] = elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n}\n\n// Pack the final SoA state back into the AoS layout expected by the host.\n__global__ void packResultKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux,\n    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n    elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = energy[i];\n    elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = tflux[i];\n}\n\n// One thread per element. Connectivity is stored transposed (connection-major,\n// conn_idx[j * n + i]) so that consecutive threads read consecutive memory.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,       // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,         // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,    // per-element material coeff\n    const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,     // per-element material flow\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux_out) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_in[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow, then add flux from all connected elements in\n    // the same order as the sequential reference to preserve rounding.\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const uint32_t neighbor = conn_idx[j * n_elems + i];\n        const val_t neighbor_energy = energy_in[neighbor];\n        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_energy) * coeff *\n                      conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    energy_out[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    tflux_out[i] = tflux_in[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU. The mesh is uploaded\n// once, all iterations run device-side with double buffering, and the final\n// state is copied back into world.elements_dynamic.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const size_t n_materials = world.materials.size();\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dynamic = d_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    val_t* d_tflux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_static, n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic)));\n sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_coeff, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_tflux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    // Ship the mesh in its native AoS layout and let CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_conn_flux));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_coeff));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_extflow));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_energy[b]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_tflux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n\n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n\n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n\n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n\n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n\n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n\n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Copy between pageable host memory and the device through a small pinned\n// double buffer. This keeps transfers on the fast DMA path without paying the\n// cost of pinning the full-size host allocations.\nclass ChunkedCopier {\npublic:\n    static constexpr size_t CHUNK_BYTES = 16 << 20;\n\n    ChunkedCopier() {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&pinned_, 2 * CHUNK_BYTES));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams_[0]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&streams_[1]));\n    }\n    ~ChunkedCopier() {\n        cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[0]);\n        cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[1]);\n        cudaFreeHost(pinned_);\n    }\n    ChunkedCopier(const ChunkedCopier&) = delete;\n    ChunkedCopier& operator=(const ChunkedCopier&) = delete;\n\n    void hostToDevice(void* dst, const void* src, size_t bytes) {\n        int b = 0;\n        for (size_t off = 0; off < bytes; off += CHUNK_BYTES, b ^= 1) {\n            const size_t c = std::min(bytes - off, CHUNK_BYTES);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b]));\n            memcpy(pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES, static_cast<const char*>(src) + off, c);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(static_cast<char*>(dst) + off,\n                                       pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES, c,\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams_[b]));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[0]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[1]));\n    }\n\n    void deviceToHost(void* dst, const void* src, size_t bytes) {\n        // Pipeline: issue async D2H into one pinned buffer while draining the\n        // other into the pageable destination.\n        size_t pending_off = 0, pending_size = 0;\n        int b = 0;\n        for (size_t off = 0; off < bytes; off += CHUNK_BYTES, b ^= 1) {\n            const size_t c = std::min(bytes - off, CHUNK_BYTES);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES,\n                                       static_cast<const char*>(src) + off, c,\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams_[b]));\n            if (pending_size > 0) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b ^ 1]));\n                memcpy(static_cast<char*>(dst) + pending_off,\n                       pinned_ + (b ^ 1) * CHUNK_BYTES, pending_size);\n            }\n            pending_off = off;\n            pending_size = c;\n        }\n        if (pending_size > 0) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b ^ 1]));\n            memcpy(static_cast<char*>(dst) + pending_off,\n                   pinned_ + (b ^ 1) * CHUNK_BYTES, pending_size);\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    char* pinned_ = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t streams_[2];\n};\n\n// Unpack the AoS mesh description into transposed (connection-major) SoA\n// arrays and resolve each element's material to per-element coefficients.\n// Runs once on the device so the host never has to restructure the mesh.\n__global__ void unpackMeshKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elements_static,\n    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,             // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,               // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,\n    val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& es = elements_static[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.num_connections);\n    num_connections[i] = n_conn;\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.connected_idx[j]);\n        conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = es.connected_flux[j];\n    }\n    const Material mat = materials[es.material_idx];\n    transfer_coeff[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n    external_flow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n    energy[i] = elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n    tflux[i] = elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n}\n\n// Pack the final SoA state back into the AoS layout expected by the host.\n__global__ void packResultKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux,\n    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n    elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = energy[i];\n    elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = tflux[i];\n}\n\n// One thread per element. Connectivity is stored transposed (connection-major,\n// conn_idx[j * n + i]) so that consecutive threads read consecutive memory.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,       // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,         // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,    // per-element material coeff\n    const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,     // per-element material flow\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux_out) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_in[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow, then add flux from all connected elements in\n    // the same order as the sequential reference to preserve rounding.\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const uint32_t neighbor = conn_idx[j * n_elems + i];\n        const val_t neighbor_energy = energy_in[neighbor];\n        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_energy) * coeff *\n                      conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    energy_out[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    tflux_out[i] = tflux_in[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU. The mesh is uploaded\n// once, all iterations run device-side with double buffering, and the final\n// state is copied back into world.elements_dynamic.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const size_t n_materials = world.materials.size();\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dynamic = d_extflow = nullptr;\n    val_t* d_energy[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    val_t* d_tflux[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_static, n_elems * sizeof(ElementStatic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dynamic, n_elems * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_materials, n_materials * sizeof(Material)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_num_conn, n_elems * CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_tflux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    // Ship the mesh in its native AoS layout and let }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n    }\n    ~ChunkedCopier() {\n        cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[0]);\n        cudaStreamDestroy(streams_[1]);\n        cudaFreeHost(pinned_);\n    }\n    ChunkedCopier(const ChunkedCopier&) = delete;\n    ChunkedCopier& operator=(const ChunkedCopier&) = delete;\n\n    void hostToDevice(void* dst, const void* src, size_t bytes) {\n        int b = 0;\n        for (size_t off = 0; off < bytes; off += CHUNK_BYTES, b ^= 1) {\n            const size_t c = std::min(bytes - off, CHUNK_BYTES);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b]));\n            memcpy(pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES, static_cast<const char*>(src) + off, c);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(static_cast<char*>(dst) + off,\n                                       pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES, c,\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, streams_[b]));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[0]));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[1]));\n    }\n\n    void deviceToHost(void* dst, const void* src, size_t bytes) {\n        // Pipeline: issue async D2H into one pinned buffer while draining the\n        // other into the pageable destination.\n        size_t pending_off = 0, pending_size = 0;\n        int b = 0;\n        for (size_t off = 0; off < bytes; off += CHUNK_BYTES, b ^= 1) {\n            const size_t c = std::min(bytes - off, CHUNK_BYTES);\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(pinned_ + b * CHUNK_BYTES,\n                                       static_cast<const char*>(src) + off, c,\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, streams_[b]));\n            if (pending_size > 0) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b ^ 1]));\n                memcpy(static_cast<char*>(dst) + pending_off,\n                       pinned_ + (b ^ 1) * CHUNK_BYTES, pending_size);\n            }\n            pending_off = off;\n            pending_size = c;\n        }\n        if (pending_size > 0) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(streams_[b ^ 1]));\n            memcpy(static_cast<char*>(dst) + pending_off,\n                   pinned_ + (b ^ 1) * CHUNK_BYTES, pending_size);\n        }\n    }\n\nprivate:\n    char* pinned_ = nullptr;\n    cudaStream_t streams_[2];\n};\n\n// Unpack the AoS mesh description into transposed (connection-major) SoA\n// arrays and resolve each element's material to per-element coefficients.\n// Runs once on the device so the host never has to restructure the mesh.\n__global__ void unpackMeshKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const ElementStatic* __restrict__ elements_static,\n    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic,\n    const Material* __restrict__ materials,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,             // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,               // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,\n    val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const ElementStatic& es = elements_static[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.num_connections);\n    num_connections[i] = n_conn;\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        conn_idx[j * n_elems + i] = static_cast<uint32_t>(es.connected_idx[j]);\n        conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] = es.connected_flux[j];\n    }\n    const Material mat = materials[es.material_idx];\n    transfer_coeff[i] = mat.transfer_coeff;\n    external_flow[i] = mat.external_flow;\n    energy[i] = elements_dynamic[i].current_energy;\n    tflux[i] = elements_dynamic[i].total_flux;\n}\n\n// Pack the final SoA state back into the AoS layout expected by the host.\n__global__ void packResultKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux,\n    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ elements_dynamic) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n    elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = energy[i];\n    elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = tflux[i];\n}\n\n// One thread per element. Connectivity is stored transposed (connection-major,\n// conn_idx[j * n + i]) so that consecutive threads read consecutive memory.\n__global__ void simulationStepKernel(\n    const size_t n_elems,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ num_connections,\n    const uint32_t* __restrict__ conn_idx,       // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ conn_flux,         // [MAX_CONNECTIONS][n_elems]\n    const val_t* __restrict__ transfer_coeff,    // per-element material coeff\n    const val_t* __restrict__ external_flow,     // per-element material flow\n    const val_t* __restrict__ energy_in,\n    const val_t* __restrict__ tflux_in,\n    val_t* __restrict__ energy_out,\n    val_t* __restrict__ tflux_out) {\n    const size_t i = blockIdx.x * (size_t)blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n_elems) return;\n\n    const val_t this_energy = energy_in[i];\n    const val_t coeff = transfer_coeff[i];\n    const uint32_t n_conn = num_connections[i];\n\n    // Start with external flow, then add flux from all connected elements in\n    // the same order as the sequential reference to preserve rounding.\n    val_t total_flux = external_flow[i];\n    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < n_conn; ++j) {\n        const uint32_t neighbor = conn_idx[j * n_elems + i];\n        const val_t neighbor_energy = energy_in[neighbor];\n        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_energy) * coeff *\n                      conn_flux[j * n_elems + i] * 0.25;\n    }\n\n    energy_out[i] = this_energy + total_flux;\n    tflux_out[i] = tflux_in[i] + fabs(total_flux);\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations on the GPU. The mesh is uploaded\n// once, all iterations run device-side with double buffering, and the final\n// state is copied back into world.elements_dynamic.\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const size_t n_materials = world.materials.size();\n\n    ElementStatic* d_static = nullptr;\n    ElementDynamic* d_dynamic = sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_idx, n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(uint32_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_conn_flux, n_elems * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_coeff, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_extflow, n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    for (int b = 0; b < 2; ++b) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_energy[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_tflux[b], n_elems * sizeof(val_t)));\n    }\n\n    // Ship the mesh in its native AoS layout and let the GPU restructure it:\n    // the device-side transpose is far faster than flattening on the host.\n    ChunkedCopier copier;\n    copier.hostToDevice(d_static, world.elements_static.data(),\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_tflux[b]));\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max
